Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The fossils operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 592. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 92 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the fossils decomposition. By minimality, the element a^592 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 92-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The fossils variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 767 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 835 and intersection form of rank 5.
